# Scripted trigger is_custom_mio_country is used to lock these countries out of generic MIO's. Remove them from this trigger and add them to the scripted trigger when doing custom MIO content

generic_infantry_equipment_organization = {
	icon = GFX_idea_generic_infantry_equipment_manufacturer_2
	name = generic_mio_general_inf_org

	icon = GFX_idea_generic_tank_manufacturer_1

	allowed = { always = no }

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 10

			factor = 0
			check_variable = { FROM.interest_rate > 8 }
		}
	}

	tree_header_text = {

		x = 2
	}

	tree_header_text = {
		text = mio_header_tank_2_eq_header
		x = 6
	}

	tree_header_text = {
		text = mio_header_reseach_and_production
		x = 10
